# House & Garden Calculators — keyword check (7 Nov 2026)

Checked against the live database (5463 tools, every category). There is no House & Garden main category yet; the home tools live in Shopping > Home Improvement Cost & Sizing and in three Business & Industrial sub-categories. No target country was given, so costs, codes and product sizes assume the US (UK keywords such as tarmac, U-value and radiator sizing are covered with metric inputs).

**Limitation:** the keyword message was cut off at 50,000 characters — it ends mid-word at "roofing cost calcul…". Everything up to that point was checked. The pasted text holds two lists, and the second largely repeats the first, so the missing part probably adds few new keywords. Please send the rest to have it checked.

## A. Summary

| | Count |
|---|---|
| Keywords submitted (received) | 6219 |
| Exact duplicates inside the list | 1680 |
| Already built (dropped) | 2135 |
| Merged (same intent) | 1805 |
| Needs review (not built) | 208 |
| **Unique new tools** | **391** |

Plus **199 existing tools** recommended to move into House & Garden (section C; not counted as new).

**Functionality the merged tools must keep:**
- Square Footage Calculator: feet + inches inputs, several rooms added together, rectangle/triangle/circle shapes, and linear-feet × width.
- Paint Calculator: walls and ceilings, doors and windows deducted, coats, and coverage presets for common brands (Sherwin-Williams, Behr, Benjamin Moore, Kilz).
- Roof Area & Squares Calculator: footprint × pitch factor, roofing squares, waste. "Roof size by address" needs satellite imagery, so this tool takes the footprint instead of looking up an address.
- Shingle Calculator: bundles, squares, starter, ridge cap, hip roofs and waste.
- Stair Calculator: rise/run to code, number of steps, stringer length, angle, and a step diagram. Deck Stair Calculator covers deck stairs and stringers.
- Duct Size Calculator: CFM ↔ size, round and rectangular ducts, velocity, friction rate, and trunk/branch lines.
- HVAC Load Calculator: a Manual J-style room-by-room estimate. Not a certified Manual J.
- Pool Chemical Calculator: chlorine, pH, alkalinity, salt and LSI in one view. The separate chlorine, salt, pH, alkalinity, shock and LSI tools stay single-purpose.
- Landscape Rock Calculator: river rock, lava rock and decorative stone densities, in yards and tons.
- Soil & Dirt Calculator: cubic yards, tons, and bags (cu ft).
- Siding Calculator: vinyl, lap, Hardie and LP SmartSide exposure presets, squares, trim and J-channel.
- Wallpaper Calculator: rolls with pattern repeat, metric and imperial roll sizes.
- Tile Calculator: floor, wall and shower, tile size, gap, waste and boxes.

**Already-built tools that need small upgrades for the merged keywords:**
- `concrete-calculator`: add a metric (UK) mode.
- `quikrete-calculator`: add Sakrete bag yields.
- `grout-calculator`: add Mapei, Laticrete and TEC product coverage.
- `generator-size-calculator`: add a Generac/Kohler wattage list.
- `deck-calculator`: add Trex/TimberTech board presets.
- `pool-size-calculator`: add an output in litres.

**Coverage gaps (suggestions, not from your list):** water heater tank vs tankless cost, attic fan size, sump pump cost, solar shade/pergola sizing, snow load by ZIP.

**Brand and app keywords** (Home Depot, Lowe's, Trex, Hardie, Generac, Mapei, Quikrete, Sakrete, Armstrong, USG, GAF, Owens Corning, Andersen, Pella and others) are merged into the generic tool as secondary keywords. Pages should not imitate a brand's own calculator.

**Dated figures to refresh:** the cost tools use typical 2026 US prices (editable). Roofing, HVAC and window prices change yearly.
